Algorithm A: Max-margin projection / subgradient:
- Normalize p sum1.
- Compute c via FFT.
- Choose active lags B = {k: c_k > threshold*(max- min) or top q}.
- Set weights λ_k = (c_k - target?)^2 for B, normalized.
- G = 2*crosscorr(λ,p).
- For multiplicative: p *= exp(-η G) (or p += -η G) then clip, renormalize.
- Occasionally add random smoothing and restarts.

def mirror_descent(a, budget_s, seed):
    n=len(a); a=clip positive, normalize sum=n.
    z=np.log(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    rng default
    z_noise = 0.0
    beta_scale=8.0; step=0.05; noise=0.05
    for each iteration until time:
       a = np.exp(z - np.mean(z)) # sum? Mean not sum; better normalize sum.
       a = a/n*a_sum_target? Simpler: a=exp(z); a = a / sum(a) * n.
       c, cmax = fast_conv(a)
       score=2*n*cmax/(n*n)=2*cmax/n (since sum normalized to n). Wait if sum exactly n, score=2*n*cmax/n^2=2*cmax/n. Good. Keep sum n.
       if score < best_score update.
       beta = beta_scale / max(1,cmax? use n? maybe beta = beta_scale/n)
       w = exp(beta*(c - cmax)); w/=sum(w)
       h = fft conv w and a? We need G=2*crosscorr. Could compute using same N maybe length 3n. Use N3 power of 2 >=3n. For n=3809 N3=16384. Fw=rfft(w,N3); Fa=rfft(a,N3); h=irfft(Fw*Fa,N3); G=2*h[n-1:3n-2]? Need length n. h length N3. G=2*h[n-1:n+n-1]. Ensure real.
       # Optional: smooth G to avoid noise: G = way? Maybe Gaussian filter? Not necessary.
       z = z - step * G
       # z scale: The mean of log a grows unbounded? We renormalize by subtracting mean? Since score scale invariant, and multiplicative updates vary sum. We can after subtract mean z to prevent overflow: z -= np.mean(z). But also a sum target n? If exp(z) average maybe not 1; renormalize later. Subtract mean okay.
       # add small noise to escape: z += noise_scale * rng.normal(0,1) if noise>0.
       # occasionally temper toward best or uniform: if no improvement, z = z*0.95 or z = (1-λ)z + λ best_z.
    return best

Let's outline final program:
Top-level docstring.
Imports: numpy as np, time, math, maybe scipy.optimize (try).
Helpers:
- next_pow2(m)
- fast_convolution(a): uses global? We can pass N.
  N=1; while N < 3*len(a)-2: N*=2 (for G also). But for score only maybe need c N >=2n; but we can compute score with N2 and G separately? To save, implement fast_score_and_gradient(a, G needed). For parameter score, only c; can use Nc=next_pow2(2n). For mirror, use Nall. We can have `fast_score(a)` computes c via FFT length 2n and returns score.
  `fast_score_c(c, a)` maybe.
- `build_param_vector(theta, basis)`: z = basis @ theta; a=exp(z - max); a/sum*n.
- `param_score(theta, n)`: uses global _BASIS? For scipy, can pass n and basis? Function signature `param_score(theta, basis, n)`.
But scipy minimize passes args; fine.
- `compute_conv_and_grad(a, N)`: returns c, G, score. Implementation:
  N pre. Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
  c = np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa, N)[:2*n-1]; c[np.abs(c)<1e-12]=0; cmax=c.max()
  sum_a=a.sum(); score=2*n*cmax/(sum_a*sum_a)
  # weights if needed: c for gradient
  return c, cmax, score
But gradient needs w; separate `compute_gradient(a,w,N)` maybe.
- `mirror_descent(a, budget_s, seed, initial_step...)`:
  Use N=next_pow2(3*n-2); a_rev = a[::-1] each iter? Need F_ar.
  For speed, precompute N, maybe a_rev.
  z = log(a) with min positive.
  best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
  step=0.05; beta_scale=8; noise=0.1; last_improve=now; stall=0.

Let's write fast functions:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p

def score_from_conv(c, a):
    s=a.sum(); cmax=c.max()
    if s<=0: return inf
    if cmax <0: cmax=0
    return 2*len(a)*cmax/(s*s)

def fast_score(a):
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c.max() < 1e-12: return inf? 
    return 2*n*c.max()/(a.sum()**2)
```
But repeated next_pow2 each call expensive; use global or pass N. In param score, basis length fixed, Nc precompute. We can make helper `fast_score_fixed(a, Nc)`.

def fast_score_fixed(a,N): ...

_BASIS=None; _N=None; _Nc=None
def param_score(theta, basis, n, N, max_z):
    z = basis.dot(theta)
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    zmax=np.max(z); zmin=np.min(z)
    rangez=zmax-zmin
    if rangez>max_z:
       z = z * (max_z/rangez)
    # maybe if all zeros? 
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = a / a.sum() * n
    # optional floor? if a max maybe > but ok
    return fast_score_fixed(a,N)
```

Basis construction:
```
def make_basis(n):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=2*x-1
    cols=[]
    cols.append(u)
    cols.append(u*u - 1/3)
    cols.append(u*u*u) # mean 0
    cols.append(u**4 - 3/5) # mean 3/5
    cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*u)) # sin(2πx)
    cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*u)) # cos(2πx)  (u=2x-1; x=(u+1)/2; 2πx=πu+π; cos = -cos(πu), sign irrelevant)
    cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.sin(3*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(3*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(u*(1-u*u)) # antisym cubic shape
    cols.append((u*u)*(1-u*u) - 0.24?) zero mean? not critical.
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    # normalize columns to max abs 1? Could scale for easier bounds.
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
       mx=np.max(np.abs(B[:,j])); if mx>0: B[:,j]/=mx
    return B
```
Using u with sin(πu) where u∈[-1,1] gives sin(πu) zero ends, antisym? sin(π(1-u))? sin(πu) at u=1 0, -1 0; sin(π(1-u))=sin(π-πu)=sin(πu), not odd? Actually centered variable u, sin(πu) is odd? sin(-x)=-sin x, yes antisym. cos(πu) symmetric. Good.
For x, u=2x-1; full period sin(2πx)=sin(πu+π)=-sinπu, sign irrelevant.

def param_coord_search(theta0,basis,n,N,seed,budget,deadline):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    theta=theta0.copy()
    cur=param_score(theta,basis,n,N)
    steps=np.full(p,0.4)
    best=theta.copy(); bests=cur
    stall=0
    while time<deadline:
       # try random coordinate
       for it in range(...):
          j=rng.integers(p)
          d=steps[j]*rng.choice([-1,1])
          theta2=theta.copy(); theta2[j]=np.clip(theta[j]+d, -1.2,1.2)
          scr=param_score(...)
          if scr < cur - 1e-9:
             theta=theta2; cur=scr; steps[j]=min(0.8, steps[j]*1.2); stall=0; update best
             break? 
       else:
          # try random line perturbation
          theta2=np.clip(theta + rng.normal(0,0.1,p), bounds)
          scr=...
          if improve: ...
          if no improve: reduce all steps *=0.8
          if steps max <0.01: break
    return best,bests
```

Mirror implementation details with exact updates:
```
def mirror_refine(a, seed, time_budget, best_score_initial):
    n=len(a); a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a[a<=0]=1e-6; a=a/a.sum()*n
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    z=np.log(a)
    z -= np.mean(z)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score_fixed(a,N) # N may longer but c OK
    step=0.05
    beta_scale=8.0
    noise=0.03
    max_range=9.0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    stall=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # exponentiate stable
       z_local = z - np.max(z)
       a = np.exp(z_local)
       a = a / a.sum() * n
       Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
       c = np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa, N)
       c = c[:2*n-1]
       c[np.abs(c) < 1e-12] = 0.0
       cmax = float(c.max())
       sum_a = float(a.sum())
       score = 2.0*n*cmax/(sum_a*sum_a)
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-10:
          best_score=score; best=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); stall=0
          # could exact evaluate if improvement significant every 200 iters? 
       else:
          stall += 1
       # weights
       beta=beta_scale / max(1.0,n)
       # if cmax <=0? no
       scaled = beta*(c - cmax)
       w = np.exp(scaled)
       wsum=w.sum()
       if wsum <=0 or not finite: w.fill(1.0/(2*n-1))
       else: w/=wsum
       Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
       # F_ar: use a[::-1]
       F_ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
       h=np.fft.irfft(Fw*F_ar, N)
       G=2.0*h[n-1:n-1+n]
       # smooth G? optional: G = 0.8*G +0.2*np.convolution? no.
       z = z - step*G
       # renormalize shape range
       z = z - np.median(z)  # median for robustness?
       zr = z.max()-z.min()
       if zr > max_range:
           z = z * (max_range/zr)
       # add noise if not improving too long
       if stall > 500:
           noise_factor = min(0.4, noise*(1+stall/1000.0))
       else: noise_factor=noise
       if noise_factor>0:
           z += noise_factor*rng.normal(size=n)
       # occasionally anneal toward best or uniform
       if stall > 2000:
           z = 0.5*z + 0.5*np.log(best)  # shape move toward best? But best may same.
           # or toward uniform zero:
           z = z * 0.7
           stall=0
       # if score explodes or not finite, reset
       if not np.isfinite(score):
           z = np.log(best) - np.mean(np.log(best))
           score=best_score; stall=0
    # final exact
    candidate=scale_for_eval(best)
    es=evaluate_sequence(candidate.tolist())
    return es, candidate
```

def random_smooth_vector(n, rng, skew):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   a = 1 + skew*rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)*(2*x-1) + skew*rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*(...)+ noise low-pass
```

```
def block_local_search(a, budget, best_score):
   n=len(a); a normalized sum n; best=a; best_score=fast...
   rng...
   while time<deadline:
      cand=best.copy()
      width=rng.integers(max(1,n//1000), max(2,n//20))
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
      scale=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05))
      cand[start:start+width]*=scale
      # maybe symmetrize? no
      cand=np.clip(cand,1e-8,1e12); cand=cand/cand.sum()*n
      score=fast_score_fixed(cand,Nc)
      if score < best_score: best=cand; best_score=score
   exact update
```

Loop:
```
while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.2:
    # normalize a from z
    z_local = z - np.max(z)
    a = np.exp(z_local)
    a_sum = a.sum()
    if not finite or a_sum<=0: reset to best, stall=0
    a = a / a_sum * n
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa, N)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.where(c > 1e-10, c, 0.0)  # ignore tiny
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <=0: score=inf
    sum_a = n (should exactly)
    score = 2.0 * n * cmax / (sum_a*sum_a)
    if np.isfinite(score):
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=score; last_update=time.monotonic(); stall=0
       else:
          stall += 1
       # adaptive step
       if score < previous_score:
          step = min(0.25, step*1.02)
       else:
          step = max(0.005, step*0.98)
       previous_score = score
    else:
       z = np.log(best); z-=mean; step*=0.5
    # weights
    beta = 12.0 / max(cmax, 1e-12)
    # if beta*c spread too small? okay
    w = np.exp(beta*(c - cmax))
    wsum = w.sum()
    if wsum <=0 or not finite: w=np.full_like(c,1.0/(len(c)))
    else: w = w/wsum
    # gradient
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    # Instead of F_ar forward, use relation to save? Let's use forward for reliability.
    F_ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * F_ar, N)
    G = 2.0 * h[n-1:n-1+n]
    # maybe smooth G by mean? Subtract mean to avoid overall log drift? z normalization handles.
    G = G - np.mean(G)  # shape only; if subtract mean, z update less scale drift. But does it preserve direction? Multiplicative with constant subtract equivalent multiplying all a by constant, removed by normalization. So safe and stabilizes.
    z = z - step * G
    # range control
    z = z - np.median(z)
    zr = z.max() - z.min()
    if not finite: reset
    if zr > max_range:
       z = z * (max_range / zr)
    # noise
    noise_step = noise
    if stall > 1000:
       noise_step = min(0.3, noise + 0.0001*stall)
    if noise_step > 0:
       z += noise_step * rng.normal(size=n)
    # anneal if huge stall
    if stall > 3000:
       # move toward best shape
       z_best = np.log(best); z_best -= np.median(z_best)
       z = 0.7*z + 0.3*z_best
       # toward uniform
       z = z * 0.8
       stall = 0
# ensure last best
best = np.clip(best,1e-12,1e12); best=best/best.sum()*n
return best, best_score
```
Subtracting mean from G: Because z update z -= step*(G - meanG) is equivalent to multiplying a by exp(-stepG) and then normalizing; yes constant factor dropped. Good. This reduces numerical drift and may speed convergence? It concentrates on relative changes.
Could G have negative values? G positive (sums of positive) but after mean subtraction negative/positive. OK.

One concern: The gradient of logsumexp plus subtracting mean may annihilate the component that changes overall load? Normalization does that. OK.

Step: If G~2, step0.08 => log change ~0.16 each iter. 1000 iters ~160 range; but range control shrinks. Good.
Noise: small.

How many iterations in 1500s? If each iteration 0.01s, 150k. Enough.

Potential improvement: Use `np.fft.rfftn`? no.

def _coord_search(theta0,basis,n,N,max_z,budget,seed):
    rng=...
    p=len(theta0)
    theta=np.asarray(theta0,float).copy()
    theta=np.clip(theta,-1.5,1.5)
    cur=_param_score(...); best=theta.copy(); bests=cur
    steps=np.full(p,0.3,float)
    deadline=start+budget
    stall=0
    while time<deadline:
       improved=False
       # try one random coordinate, and maybe all if time?
       for _ in range(3):
           j=int(rng.integers(p)); delta=steps[j]*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
           theta2=theta.copy(); theta2[j]=np.clip(theta[j]+delta,-1.5,1.5)
           scr=_param_score(...)
           if scr < cur - 1e-12:
              theta=theta2; cur=scr; steps[j]=min(0.8, steps[j]*1.2); improved=True; break
       # random line
       theta2=np.clip(theta + rng.normal(0,0.08,p), -1.5,1.5)
       scr=...
       if scr < cur -1e-12: improved=True; update
       if improved:
          if scr < bests: best,bests=theta,cur
          stall=0
       else:
          steps*=0.85; stall+=1
          if np.max(steps)<1e-3: break
    return best,bests
```

Random skew vector generation helper top-level:
```
def _random_skew_seed(n, rng, skew_strength=0.8):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   u=2*x-1
   z= skew_strength*u + 0.3*rng.normal(size=n)
   # optional add quadratic
   z += 0.4*rng.uniform(-1,1)*(u*u-1/3)
   a=np.exp(np.tanh(z))
   return a/a.sum()*n
```

"""Step-function coefficient search..."""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAS_SCIPY=True
except Exception:
    HAS_SCIPY=False

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=max(1,int(m))
    p=1
    while p<m:
       p <<=1
    return p
```

`_fast_score_fixed`
```
def _fast_score_fixed(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return float('inf')
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: return float('inf')
    np.maximum(c,0.0,c)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0.0: return float('inf')
    return float((2.0*n*cmax)/(s*s))
```
Could modifying c in place from irfft? `irfft` returns new, okay. `np.maximum(c,0.0,c)` works.
Need N at least 2n-1. `_fast_score` uses N=next_pow2(2*n-1).

`_make_basis`:
```
def _make_basis(n):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=2.0*x-1.0
    cols=[u]
    cols.append(u*u - 1.0/3.0)
    cols.append(u**3)
    cols.append(u**4 - 3.0/5.0)
    cols.append(np.sin(np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.sin(3*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(3*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.sin(4*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(np.cos(4*np.pi*u))
    cols.append(u*(1.0-u*u))
    cols.append((u*u)*(1.0-u*u))
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
       mx=float(np.max(np.abs(B[:,j])))
       if mx>1e-12: B[:,j]/=mx
       else: B[:,j]=0
    return B
```
Maybe `u*u*u` better than `u**3`. Fine.

`_param_to_vector`:
```
def _param_to_vector(theta,basis,n,max_z=6.0):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    theta=np.clip(theta,-1.6,1.6)
    raw=basis.dot(theta)
    raw=raw - float(np.mean(raw))
    z=max_z * np.tanh(raw / max_z)
    # remove drift mean
    z=z - float(np.mean(z))
    z_local=z - float(np.max(z))
    a=np.exp(z_local)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    else:
       a=a/s*n
    return a
```
If n maybe not basis length? Ensure. Good.

`_param_score` calls vector and fast score; but each call constructs vector. Pass N produced with 2n.
```
def _param_score(theta,basis,n,N,max_z=6.0):
    a=_param_to_vector(theta,basis,n,max_z)
    return _fast_score_fixed(a,N)
```

`_param_search`:
```
def _param_search(basis,n,N,budget_s,seed):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(0.0,budget_s)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=basis.shape[1]
    max_z=6.0
    structured=[]
    zeros=np.zeros(p)
    for v in (-1.2,-0.8,-0.5,-0.3,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.2):
       t=zeros.copy(); t[0]=v; structured.append(t.copy())
    for v in (-1.0,-0.6,-0.3,0.3,0.6,1.0):
       t=zeros.copy(); t[0]=v; t[1]=0.4 if v>0 else -0.4; structured.append(t.copy())
    t=zeros.copy(); t[0]=0.8; t[1]=-0.3; t[4]=0.3; structured.append(t.copy())
    t=zeros.copy(); t[0]=0.8; t[2]=0.4; t[6]=0.2; structured.append(t.copy())
    # random
    starts=structured
    max_random=300
    for _ in range(max_random):
       starts.append(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0,p))
    scores=[]
    for t in starts:
       sc=_param_score(t,basis,n,N,max_z)
       if not math.isfinite(sc): sc=1e100
       scores.append(sc)
    best_theta=starts[0]; best_score=scores[0]
    for i in range(len(starts)):
       if scores[i] < best_score: best_score=scores[i]; best_theta=starts[i]
    # top local
    arr_scores=np.array(scores)
    order=np.argsort(arr_scores)
    local_count=min(8,len(order))
    local_baskept? 
    local_budget_total=deadline-time.monotonic()
    # measure eval time
    t0=time.monotonic(); _param_score(best_theta,basis,n,N,max_z); eval_dt=max(1e-6,time.monotonic()-t0)
    per_start=max(5.0, local_budget_total/max(1,local_count)*0.7)
    maxiter=int(min(1200, max(200, per_start/(3.0*eval_dt))))
    for li in range(local_count):
       idx=int(order[li])
       theta0=starts[idx].copy(); cur=scores[idx]
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       if HAS_SCIPY:
          try:
             res=minimize(_param_score, theta0, args=(basis,n,N,max_z), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-8,'adaptive':True})
             if hasattr(res,'x'):
                theta0=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
                sc=math.isfinite(res.fun) and float(res.fun) or cur
                if math.isfinite(res.fun) and res.fun<cur:
                   theta0=np.clip(theta0,-1.6,1.6)
                   sc=float(res.fun)
                else:
                   sc=cur
             else: sc=cur
          except Exception:
             theta0,cur=_coord_search(theta0,basis,n,N,max_z,min(60.0,per_start),seed+li)
       else:
          theta0,cur=_coord_search(theta0,basis,n,N,max_z,min(120.0,per_start),seed+li)
       if math.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
          best_score=cur; best_theta=theta0
       print("param candidate score %.6f" % cur)
    a=_param_to_vector(best_theta,basis,n,max_z)
    return a,best_score,best_theta
```

`_mirror_refine`: We'll code with N_all. For `a` input normalized but we normalize. Use `budget_s` maybe 0 skip.
```
def _mirror_refine(a, seed, budget_s):
    if budget_s <= 0: return a, _fast_score(a)
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=a.size
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64); s=n
    a=a/s*n
    N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    freqs=np.arange(N//2+1,dtype=np.float64)
    phase=np.exp(-1j*(2.0*np.pi*(n-1)/N)*freqs)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
    best=a.copy()
    best_score=_fast_score_fixed(a,N)
    prev_score=best_score
    step=0.08
    beta_scale=12.0
    noise=0.02
    max_range=9.0
    stall=0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,budget_s)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
       it +=1
       z_local=z - float(np.max(z))
       a=np.exp(z_local)
       s=float(a.sum())
       if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
          z=np.log(best); z -= float(np.mean(z)); stall=0; continue
       a=a/s*n
       F_a=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(F_a*F_a,N)
       c=c[:2*n-1]
       np.maximum(c,0.0,c)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       sum_a=float(a.sum())
       if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax<=0.0:
          score=float('inf')
       else:
          score=float((2.0*n*cmax)/(sum_a*sum_a))
       if math.isfinite(score):
           if score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=score; best=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); stall=0
               if best_score < 1.5:
                   print("mirror iter %d score %.6f" % (it,best_score))
           else:
               stall += 1
           if score < prev_score:
               step=min(0.25, step*1.02)
           else:
               step=max(0.004, step*0.985)
           prev_score=score
       else:
           z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z -= float(np.mean(z)); step*=0.5
       # weights
       beta=beta_scale / max(cmax, 1e-12)
       scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
       # To prevent extreme underflow maybe scaled = np.maximum(scaled, -50)
       w=np.exp(scaled)
       wsum=float(w.sum())
       if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <=0:
           w=np.full(c.shape, 1.0/float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
       else:
           w=w/wsum
       F_w=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
       F_ar=F_a.conj()*phase
       h=np.fft.irfft(F_w*F_ar,N)
       G=2.0*h[n-1:n-1+n]
       if not math.isfinite(G).all():
           G=np.zeros(n)
       G=G - float(np.mean(G))
       z=z - step*G
       z=z - float(np.mean(z))
       zr=float(np.max(z)-np.min(z))
       if not math.isfinite(zr):
           z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
       else:
           if zr > max_range:
               z = z * (max_range/zr)
       # noise
       noise_step=noise
       if stall > 1000:
           noise_step=min(0.25, noise + 0.00005*stall)
       if noise_step >0:
           z = z + noise_step * rng.normal(size=n)
       # anneal
       if stall > 3000:
           zb=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); zb -= float(np.mean(zb))
           z = 0.7*z + 0.3*zb
           z = z * 0.8
           stall = 0
    best=np.where(np.isfinite(best),best,0.0); best=np.maximum(best,0.0)
    bs=best.sum()
    if bs>0: best=best/bs*n
    else: best=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    return best,best_score
```

if best_score < 1.505 and (it % 5000 ==0 or score < best_score - ...): print

def _block_local(a, seed, budget_s):
    a=np.asarray(a,...); n=a.size; N=_next_pow2(2*n-1); normalize; best=a; best_score=fastfixed;
    rng=...
    deadline=...
    minw=max(1,n//4000); maxw=max(minw+1,n//20)
    while time<deadline-0.2:
       cand=best.copy()
       width=int(rng.integers(minw,maxw))
       if width<n:
          start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
          scale=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.04)))
          cand[start:start+width] *= scale
          cand=np.where(np.isfinite(cand),cand,0); cand=np.maximum(cand,0)
          cs=cand.sum()
          if cs>0: cand=cand/cs*n
          sc=_fast_score_fixed(cand,N)
          if math.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
    return best,best_score
```

`_upsample`:
```
def _upsample(a,n):
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(new,old,a)
   b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0 b=b/s*n
   else b=np.ones(n)
   return b
```
May not use.

`_get_initial`:
```
def _get_initial(default_n=3809):
    n=default_n; base=None
    h=None
    try:
       h=height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
       h=None
    if h is not None:
       try:
          arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64)
          if arr.ndim !=1: arr=arr.ravel()
          arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
          arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
          if arr.size>0 and arr.sum()>0:
             if arr.size <= 8192:
                n=arr.size; base=arr/arr.sum()*n
             else:
                n=8192; base=_upsample(arr,n)
       except Exception:
          base=None
    if base is None:
       base=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    return n,base
```

`_scale_for_eval`: top-level.
```
def _scale_for_eval(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(1)
   mx=float(a.max())
   if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx<=0:
      return np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)*1.0
   a=a*(1000.0/mx)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   mx=float(a.max())
   if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx<=0:
      return np.ones(a.size)*1.0
   if mx>1000.0:
      a=a*(1000.0/mx)
   return a
```
If a.size=3809 and min very small, no issue.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    docstring? 
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    deadline=start+max(1.0,total)-5.0
    n, base = _get_initial(3809)
    Nc=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_vec=base.copy()
    best_internal=_fast_score_fixed(base,Nc)
    best_exact=_exact_score_of(base)
    print("n=%d initial exact=%.6f internal=%.6f" % (n,best_exact,best_internal))
    # Stage 1 parameter
    basis=_make_basis(n)
    param_budget=min(360.0, max(30.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.18))
    param_vec, param_score, param_theta = _param_search(basis,n,Nc,param_budget,seed)
    if math.isfinite(param_score) and param_score < best_internal:
       best_vec=param_vec; best_internal=param_score
       best_exact=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
    print("after param best=%.6f exact=%.6f" % (best_internal,best_exact))
    # Build seeds
    seeds=[]; weights=[]
    seeds.append(best_vec); weights.append(0.55)
    # add top param starts? We don't have list. Could generate a couple by perturbing theta.
    rng0=np.random.default_rng(seed+1)
    for k in range(2):
       th=param_theta + rng0.normal(0,0.2,param_theta.size)
       v=_param_to_vector(th,basis,n,6.0)
       sc=_fast_score_fixed(v,Nc)
       if math.isfinite(sc): seeds.append(v); weights.append(0.12)
    # base from global if different and not too bad
    v=base.copy()
    if not np.allclose(v,best_vec):
       sc=_fast_score_fixed(v,Nc)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_internal + 0.5:
           seeds.append(v); weights.append(0.08)
    # random skew
    v=_random_skew_seed(n, np.random.default_rng(seed+2))
    sc=_fast_score_fixed(v,Nc)
    if math.isfinite(sc): seeds.append(v); weights.append(0.08)
    # normalize weights sum 1
    wsum=sum(weights); if wsum>0: weights=[w/wsum for w in weights] else ...
    # local budgets: reserve 15% for local
    local_budget=min(180.0, max(0.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())*0.15))
    mirror_total=max(60.0, (deadline-time.monotonic()) - local_budget - 10.0)
    # run mirrors
    for i,v in enumerate(seeds):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline-60: break
        budget=mirror_total*weights[i]
        if budget < 20: budget=20? but could overrun. Use if remaining > 30.
        if budget <=0: continue
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        budget=min(budget, max(0.0, rem-30.0))
        if budget < 30: continue
        print("mirror seed %d budget %.0f" % (i,budget))
        vb, scb = _mirror_refine(v, seed+100+i, budget)
        scb_internal=_fast_score_fixed(vb,Nc)
        if math.isfinite(scb_internal) and scb_internal < best_internal:
            best_vec=vb; best_internal=scb_internal
            best_exact=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
        elif math.isfinite(scb) and scb < best_internal:
            # use returned score? but recompute
            best_vec=vb; best_internal=scb; best_exact=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
        print("after mirror %d internal=%.6f exact=%.6f" % (i,best_internal,best_exact))
    # local
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    local_budget=min(local_budget, max(0.0, rem-20.0))
    if local_budget > 30:
       print("local budget %.0f" % local_budget)
       vb, scb = _block_local(best_vec, seed+999, local_budget)
       scb_internal=_fast_score_fixed(vb,Nc)
       if math.isfinite(scb_internal) and scb_internal < best_internal:
           best_vec=vb; best_internal=scb_internal
       best_exact=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
    # final exact update
    ef=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
    if math.isfinite(ef): best_exact=ef
    else:
       # fallback uniform
       best_vec=np.ones(n); best_exact=_exact_score_of(best_vec)
    print("final internal=%.6f exact=%.6f n=%d" % (best_internal,best_exact,n))
    scaled=_scale_for_eval(best_vec)
    return [float(x) for x in scaled]

`_random_skew_seed` needs definition:
```
def _random_skew_seed(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); u=2*x-1
    amp=float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.2))
    q=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5))
    raw=amp*u + q*(u*u-1/3)
    raw += 0.2*rng.normal(size=n)
    a=np.exp(np.tanh(raw))
    s=a.sum(); if s>0 a=a/s*n else ones
    return a
```

```python
"""
Search procedure for non-negative step heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)**2

Strategies:
1) A low-dimensional global search over smooth positive log-densities
   (trigonometric/polynomial basis).
2) A full-dimensional mirror-descent refinement in log-space using a
   log-sum-exp approximation of the max convolution term and FFT-based
   gradients.
3) A light local block-polish stage.
The best exact-evaluated candidate found is returned at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SCIPY = False


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _fast_score_fixed(a, N):
    """Fast convolution-based objective using a precomputed FFT length N."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    c = c[: 2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, c)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float((2.0 * n * cmax) / (s * s))


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    return _fast_score_fixed(a, _next_pow2(2 * n - 1))


def _make_basis(n):
    """Fixed smooth basis for a low-dimensional log-density parametrization."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    cols = [
        u,
        u * u - 1.0 / 3.0,
        u * u * u,
        u * u * u * u - 3.0 / 5.0,
        np.sin(np.pi * u),
        np.cos(np.pi * u),
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * u),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * u),
        np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * u),
        np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * u),
        np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * u),
        np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * u),
        u * (1.0 - u * u),
        (u * u) * (1.0 - u * u),
    ]
    B = np.column_stack(cols)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(B[:, j])))
        if mx > 1e-12:
            B[:, j] /= mx
        else:
            B[:, j] = 0.0
    return B


def _param_to_vector(theta, basis, n, max_z=6.0):
    """Convert low-dimensional parameters to a normalized positive vector."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    theta = np.clip(theta, -1.6, 1.6)
    raw = basis.dot(theta)
    raw = raw - float(np.mean(raw))
    z = max_z * np.tanh(raw / max_z)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s * n
    return a


def _param_score(theta, basis, n, N, max_z=6.0):
    a = _param_to_vector(theta, basis, n, max_z)
    return _fast_score_fixed(a, N)


def _coord_search(theta0, basis, n, N, max_z, budget, seed):
    """Simple bounded coordinate/random-line local search fallback."""
    theta0 = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = theta0.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cur = _param_score(theta0, basis, n, N, max_z)
    best = theta0.copy()
    best_score = cur
    steps = np.full(p, 0.3, dtype=np.float64)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.1:
        improved = False

        for _ in range(4):
            j = int(rng.integers(p))
            delta = steps[j] * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0)
            theta2 = theta0.copy()
            theta2[j] = np.clip(theta0[j] + delta, -1.6, 1.6)
            sc = _param_score(theta2, basis, n, N, max_z)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
                theta0, cur = theta2, sc
                steps[j] = min(0.9, steps[j] * 1.15)
                improved = True
                break

        theta2 = np.clip(theta0 + 0.10 * rng.normal(size=p), -1.6, 1.6)
        sc = _param_score(theta2, basis, n, N, max_z)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur - 1e-12:
            theta0, cur = theta2, sc
            improved = True

        if improved:
            if cur < best_score:
                best, best_score = theta0.copy(), cur
            steps *= 1.02
            np.clip(steps, None, 0.9, out=steps)
        else:
            steps *= 0.82
            if float(np.max(steps)) < 1e-3:
                break

    return best, best_score


def _param_search(basis, n, N, budget, seed):
    """Global low-dimensional flow search."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = int(basis.shape[1])
    max_z = 6.0

    starts = []
    zeros = np.zeros(p, dtype=np.float64)

    for v in (-1.2, -0.8, -0.5, -0.3, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2):
        t = zeros.copy()
        t[0] = v
        starts.append(t.copy())

    for v in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        t = zeros.copy()
        t[0] = v
        t[1] = 0.4 if v > 0 else -0.4
        starts.append(t.copy())

    t = zeros.copy()
    t[0] = 0.8
    t[1] = -0.3
    t[4] = 0.3
    starts.append(t.copy())

    t = zeros.copy()
    t[0] = 0.8
    t[2] = 0.4
    t[6] = 0.2
    starts.append(t.copy())

    # Broad random starts.
    max_random = 800
    for _ in range(max_random):
        starts.append(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, p).astype(np.float64))

    scores = []
    for t in starts:
        sc = _param_score(t, basis, n, N, max_z)
        scores.append(sc if math.isfinite(sc) else 1e100)

    best_theta = starts[0].copy()
    best_score = scores[0]
    for i in range(len(starts)):
        if scores[i] < best_score:
            best_score = scores[i]
            best_theta = starts[i].copy()

    local_count = min(10, len(starts))
    arr_scores = np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(arr_scores)

    # Calibrate one evaluation.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    _param_score(best_theta, basis, n, N, max_z)
    eval_dt = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - t0)

    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    per_start = max(5.0, 0.7 * remaining / max(1, local_count))
    maxiter = int(min(1200, max(200, per_start / (3.0 * eval_dt))))

    for li in range(local_count):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        idx = int(order[li])
        theta0 = starts[idx].copy()
        cur = scores[idx]
        if not math.isfinite(cur):
            continue

        if HAS_SCIPY:
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _param_score,
                    theta0,
                    args=(basis, n, N, max_z),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": maxiter,
                        "xatol": 1e-5,
                        "fatol": 1e-8,
                        "adaptive": True,
                    },
                )
                fun = float(res.fun)
                if math.isfinite(fun) and fun < cur:
                    theta0 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    theta0 = np.clip(theta0, -1.6, 1.6)
                    cur = fun
            except Exception:
                theta0, cur = _coord_search(
                    theta0, basis, n, N, max_z,
                    min(60.0, per_start),
                    seed + li
                )
        else:
            theta0, cur = _coord_search(theta0, basis, n, N, max_z, per_start, seed + li)

        if math.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best_theta = theta0.copy()

    a = _param_to_vector(best_theta, basis, n, max_z)
    return a, best_score, best_theta


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == int(n):
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        b = b / s * int(n)
    else:
        b = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return b


def _get_initial(default_n=3809):
    n = default_n
    base = None

    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        h = None

    if h is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim != 1:
                arr = arr.ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size >= 64 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                if arr.size <= 8192:
                    n = int(arr.size)
                    base = arr / float(arr.sum()) * n
                else:
                    n = 8192
                    base = _upsample(arr, n)
        except Exception:
            base = None

    if base is None:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return n, base


def _scale_for_eval(a):
    """Scale so the evaluator's clip-to-1000 rule is not active."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    mx = float(a.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (1000.0 / mx)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    mx = float(a.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if mx > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / mx)
    return a


def _exact_score_of(a):
    try:
        scaled = _scale_for_eval(a)
        val = evaluate_sequence(scaled.tolist())
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def _random_skew_seed(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.2))
    q = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
    raw = amp * u + q * (u * u - 1.0 / 3.0)
    raw += 0.15 * rng.normal(size=n)
    a = np.exp(np.tanh(raw))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s * n
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _mirror_refine(a, seed, budget):
    """
    Full-dimensional log-space descent.

    The objective is approximated by a log-sum-exp of convolution lags.
    Gradients are computed with FFTs.
    """
    if budget <= 0.0:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a / s * n

    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    freqs = np.arange(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    phase = np.exp(-1j * (2.0 * math.pi * float(n - 1) / float(N)) * freqs)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_fixed(a, N)
    prev_score = best_score
    step = 0.12
    beta_scale = 12.0
    noise = 0.018
    max_abs_z = 8.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget)
    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
        it += 1
        z = z - float(np.max(z))
        a = np.exp(z)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            continue
        a = a / s * n

        F_a = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F_a * F_a, N)
        c = c[: 2 * n - 1]
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, c)
        cmax = float(c.max())

        if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            step = max(0.004, step * 0.75)
            stall += 1
            continue

        sum_a = float(a.sum())
        score = float((2.0 * n * cmax) / (sum_a * sum_a))

        if math.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best = a.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            if score < prev_score:
                step = min(0.35, step * 1.02)
            else:
                step = max(0.004, step * 0.985)
            prev_score = score

        beta = beta_scale / max(cmax, 1e-12)
        w = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / wsum

        F_w = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        F_ar = F_a.conj() * phase
        h = np.fft.irfft(F_w * F_ar, N)
        G = 2.0 * h[n - 1 : n - 1 + n]

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            G = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        G = G - float(np.mean(G))
        G = np.clip(G, -10.0, 10.0)

        z = z - step * G
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        zmax = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
        if not math.isfinite(zmax):
            z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            step *= 0.75
        elif zmax > max_abs_z:
            z = z * (max_abs_z / zmax)

        noise_step = noise
        if stall > 1200:
            noise_step = min(0.22, noise + 5e-5 * stall)
        if noise_step > 0.0:
            z += noise_step * rng.normal(size=n)

        if stall > 3500:
            zb = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            zb -= float(np.mean(zb))
            z = 0.75 * z + 0.25 * zb
            z *= 0.8
            stall = 0

    best = np.where(np.isfinite(best), best, 0.0)
    best = np.maximum(best, 0.0)
    bs = float(best.sum())
    if bs > 0.0:
        best = best / bs * n
    else:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return best, best_score


def _block_local(a, seed, budget):
    if budget <= 0.0:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    a = a / s * n

    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_fixed(a, N)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget)
    minw = max(1, n // 4000)
    maxw = max(minw + 1, n // 20)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
        cand = best.copy()
        width = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw))
        if width < n:
            pos = int(rng.integers(0, n - width))
            scale = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04)))
            cand[pos : pos + width] *= scale
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs > 0.0:
                cand = cand / cs * n
                sc = _fast_score_fixed(cand, N)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = sc

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    guard = min(10.0, 0.02 * total)
    deadline = start_time + total - guard

    n, base = _get_initial(3809)
    Nc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best_vec = base.copy()
    best_internal = _fast_score_fixed(best_vec, Nc)
    best_exact = _exact_score_of(best_vec)

    print("initial n=%d internal=%.6f exact=%.6f" % (n, best_internal, best_exact))

    # Stage 1: low-dimensional global flow search.
    basis = _make_basis(n)
    param_budget = min(360.0, max(20.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.20))
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0 and param_budget > 20.0:
        try:
            param_vec, param_score, param_theta = _param_search(
                basis, n, Nc, param_budget, int(seed)
            )
            if math.isfinite(param_score) and param_score < best_internal:
                best_vec = param_vec
                best_internal = param_score
                best_exact = _exact_score_of(best_vec)
        except Exception:
            param_vec, param_score, param_theta = base.copy(), best_internal, np.zeros(basis.shape[1])

    print("after param search internal=%.6f exact=%.6f" % (best_internal, best_exact))

    # Prepare seeds for mirror descent.
    seeds = []
    weights = []
    seeds.append(best_vec)
    weights.append(0.55)

    rng0 = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1)
    if isinstance(param_theta, np.ndarray):
        for _ in range(2):
            th = np.asarray(param_theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            th += 0.20 * rng0.normal(size=th.size)
            v = _param_to_vector(th, basis, n, 6.0)
            sc = _fast_score_fixed(v, Nc)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                seeds.append(v)
                weights.append(0.11)

    base_score = _fast_score_fixed(base, Nc)
    if math.isfinite(base_score) and base_score < best_internal + 0.6:
        seeds.append(base.copy())
        weights.append(0.08)

    skew = _random_skew_seed(n, np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 2))
    skew_score = _fast_score_fixed(skew, Nc)
    if math.isfinite(skew_score):
        seeds.append(skew)
        weights.append(0.08)

    wsum = 0.0
    for w in weights:
        wsum += float(w)
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        norm_weights = [1.0 / max(1, len(weights))] * len(weights)
    else:
        norm_weights = [float(w) / wsum for w in weights]
    weights = norm_weights

    # Reserve a little time for final polishing.
    local_reserve = min(180.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.15))
    mirror_total = max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - local_reserve - 10.0)

    for i, v in enumerate(seeds):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        b = mirror_total * weights[i]
        b = min(b, max(0.0, rem - 30.0))
        if b < 30.0:
            continue
        print("mirror seed %d budget=%.0fs" % (i, b))
        try:
            vv, sc = _mirror_refine(v, int(seed) + 100 + i, b)
            sc2 = _fast_score_fixed(vv, Nc)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_internal:
                best_vec = vv
                best_internal = sc2
                best_exact = _exact_score_of(best_vec)
        except Exception:
            pass
        print("after seed %d best_internal=%.6f exact=%.6f" % (i, best_internal, best_exact))

    # Final local block polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    lb = min(local_reserve, max(0.0, rem - 20.0))
    if lb > 30.0:
        print("polish budget=%.0fs" % lb)
        try:
            vv, sc = _block_local(best_vec, int(seed) + 999, lb)
            sc2 = _fast_score_fixed(vv, Nc)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_internal:
                best_vec = vv
                best_internal = sc2
        except Exception:
            pass

    exact_final = _exact_score_of(best_vec)
    if math.isfinite(exact_final):
        best_exact = exact_final
    else:
        best_vec = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_exact = _exact_score_of(best_vec)

    print("final best internal=%.6f exact=%.6f n=%d" % (best_internal, best_exact, n))

    scaled = _scale_for_eval(best_vec)
    return [float(x) for x in scaled]
```
